__ test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts[index_dtype0-value_dtype0-4] __

blocks = 4, value_dtype = torch.float32, index_dtype = torch.int32

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

blocks = 129, value_dtype = torch.float32, index_dtype = torch.int32

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

__ test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts[index_dtype0-value_dtype1-3] __

blocks = 3, value_dtype = torch.float64, index_dtype = torch.int32

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

__ test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts[index_dtype0-value_dtype1-4] __

blocks = 4, value_dtype = torch.float64, index_dtype = torch.int32

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

blocks = 127, value_dtype = torch.float64, index_dtype = torch.int32

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

blocks = 129, value_dtype = torch.float64, index_dtype = torch.int32

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

__ test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts[index_dtype1-value_dtype0-3] __

blocks = 3, value_dtype = torch.float32, index_dtype = torch.int64

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

__ test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts[index_dtype1-value_dtype0-4] __

blocks = 4, value_dtype = torch.float32, index_dtype = torch.int64

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

_ test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts[index_dtype1-value_dtype0-127] _

blocks = 127, value_dtype = torch.float32, index_dtype = torch.int64

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

blocks = 129, value_dtype = torch.float32, index_dtype = torch.int64

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

__ test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts[index_dtype1-value_dtype1-3] __

blocks = 3, value_dtype = torch.float64, index_dtype = torch.int64

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

__ test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts[index_dtype1-value_dtype1-4] __

blocks = 4, value_dtype = torch.float64, index_dtype = torch.int64

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

_ test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts[index_dtype1-value_dtype1-127] _

blocks = 127, value_dtype = torch.float64, index_dtype = torch.int64

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )

blocks = 129, value_dtype = torch.float64, index_dtype = torch.int64

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("blocks", [3, 4, 127, 129])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("value_dtype", [torch.float32, torch.float64])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("index_dtype", [torch.int32, torch.int64])
    def test_insert_resampled_exact_tokens_and_counts(blocks, value_dtype, index_dtype):
        """DS41F partials and TP3/TP4 winners preserve ties, skips and view padding."""
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.rejection_sampler_utils import (
            _insert_resampled_kernel,
        )
        from vllm.v1.worker.gpu.spec_decode.tilelang_resampled import insert_resampled
    
        torch.manual_seed(7319)
        n = 17
        widths = torch.randint(1, 7, (n,), device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32)
        counts = torch.arange(n, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int32) % widths
        saved_counts = counts.clone()
        cu = torch.cat((widths.new_zeros(1), widths.cumsum(0).int()))
        states = torch.randperm(n + 11, device="cuda")[:n].to(index_dtype)
        mapping = states.repeat_interleave(widths.long())
        temp = torch.ones(n + 11, device="cuda")
        temp[states[9::2].long()] = 0
        values_storage = torch.full(
            (n, blocks + 5), 123.0, device="cuda", dtype=value_dtype
        )
        values = values_storage[:, 2 : blocks + 2]
        values.normal_()
        values[0] = float("nan")
        values[1] = -float("inf")
        values[2] = -1
        values[2, 0] = values[2, -1] = 10
        values[3] = float("nan")
        values[3, blocks // 2] = -7
        values[4, 0] = values[4, -1] = float("inf")
        values[5] = -float("inf")
        values[5, 0], values[5, -1] = -0.0, 0.0
        values[6] = 1
        values[6, -1] = 1 + (2**-40 if value_dtype == torch.float64 else 2**-20)
        values[7] = 0
        values[7, -1] = torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        values[8] = -1
        values[8, -1] = -torch.finfo(value_dtype).tiny / 2
        ids_storage = torch.full((n, blocks + 3), -777, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        ids = ids_storage[:, 1 : blocks + 1]
        ids.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, ids.shape, device="cuda"))
        sampled_storage = torch.full((n, 9), -999, device="cuda", dtype=torch.int64)
        sampled = sampled_storage[:, 1:7]
        sampled.copy_(torch.randint(0, 129280, sampled.shape, device="cuda"))
        saved = sampled_storage.clone()
        expected = sampled.clone()
        clean = torch.where(values.isnan(), -float("inf"), values)
        winners = ids.gather(1, clean.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
        should_insert = (temp[states.long()] != 0) | (counts == widths - 1)
        rows = torch.arange(n, device="cuda")[should_insert]
        expected[rows, counts[rows].long()] = winners[rows]
        original_storage = saved.clone()
        original = original_storage[:, 1:7]
        original_counts = counts.clone()
        _insert_resampled_kernel[(n,)](
            original,
            original.stride(0),
            original_counts,
            ids,
            ids.stride(0),
            values,
            values.stride(0),
            blocks,
            cu,
            mapping,
            temp,
            PADDED_RESAMPLE_NUM_BLOCKS=1 << (blocks - 1).bit_length(),
        )
